   Salon at `salon-conversation.ts:1323`. The board's query takes
   `refetchInterval: this.realtime.refetchInterval(60_000)`.
8. **`turnMode` on the wire is NEVER null** (W1; v4 `handlers/get.ts:378 ??
   'rotation'`), so the SPA's `Chat` type is `turnMode: 'rotation' |
   'storyteller'` and the stand-in behaviour on main's server (the key
   ABSENT) must read as `'rotation'` — the board hidden, the radiogroup on
   `normal` — through ONE `turnModeOf(chat) = chat.turnMode ?? 'rotation'`
   read, never a scatter of `?? 'rotation'`.

### v4 at the pin — the board (`components/chat/StorytellerBoard.tsx`, 431 lines; every byte)

- **Data:** `useQuery(queryKeys.chats.storytellerRound(chatId))` → `GET
  ?action=storyteller-round` → `{ round: StorytellerRound | null }`;
  `refetchInterval = useRealtimeRefetchInterval(60_000)` (`:52`
  `FALLBACK_POLL_MS`). `round === null` → renders NOTHING (`:272`).
- **Selection / arrangement reset on `roundId` change** (`:191-195`):
  `selected = new Set()`, `arrangement = null`.
- **Mutations:** `ask` (`:201-210`) POSTs `?action=storyteller-ask`
  `{roundId, participantId}`; `onError` toast
  `apiErrorMessage(error, 'Could not ask for that bid')`; `onSettled`
  invalidates the round key. `lie` (`:212-221`) POSTs `?action=storyteller-lie`
  `{roundId}`; `onError` `'Could not close the round'`; `onSettled`
  invalidates.
- **Derived:** `cards = arrangeBids(round.bids, arrangement)` (`:223`);
  `biddable = cards.filter(status === 'bid')`; `chosenBids = biddable.filter
  (selected.has(id))` — IN BOARD ORDER; `arranged = arrangement !== null`;
  `destroyChosen = chosenBids.some(tier === 'destroy')`; `destroyMixed =
  destroyChosen && chosenBids.length > 1` (`:238-239`).
- **`toggle(bid)`** (`:241-254`): already ticked → untick; a `destroy` bid →
  the selection becomes ONLY it; else every ticked destroy is dropped and the
  bid added. **`selectAll`** (`:257-259`): every biddable whose `tier !==
  'destroy'`. **`run(order)`** (`:261-270`): no round or nothing chosen →
  return; `running = true`; `await onRun(roundId, chosenBids.map(id), order,
  arranged)`; `finally` `running = false` + invalidate.
- **The closed-round summary** (`:275-289`): `names = round.selection?.names
  ?? []`; `status === 'running'` → `` `Speaking now: ${names.join(', ')}` ``;
  `closedReason === 'let-lie'` → `The last round was let lie.`; `'superseded'`
  or `'abandoned'` → `The last round closed unrun.`; else `names.length > 0 ?
  `` `Last round: ${names.join(', ')}${round.selection?.order === 'simultaneous'
  ? ' (all at once)' : ''}` `` : 'The last round has run.'`; rendered as
  `<div class="qt-storyteller-summary" role="status"><Icon name="users"
  class="w-3.5 h-3.5"/><span>{text}</span></div>`.
- **The open board** (`:294-430`): `pending = bids.filter(status ===
  'pending').length`; `disabled = busy || running`. `<section
  class="qt-storyteller-board" aria-label="The storyteller's board">`; header
  `<div class="qt-storyteller-board-header"><span>The company bids{pending >
  0 && <span class="qt-storyteller-meta"> — {pending} still considering…
  </span>}</span><span class="qt-storyteller-board-tools">[Reset order][Select
  all]</span></div>` — `Reset order` (`qt-button-ghost qt-button-sm`, only
  when `arranged`, `disabled`, title `Put the cards back and let the room's
  rotation set the order`), `Select all` (`qt-button-ghost qt-button-sm`,
  `disabled || biddable.length === 0`).
- **Per card** (between the header and `:387`), class `cardClass(bid, selected, draggable,
  dragging)` (`:78-87`): `qt-storyteller-card` + `-selected` + `-draggable` +
  `-dragging` + `-act` (bid & tier act) + `-destroy` (bid & tier destroy) +
  `-quiet` (status neither `bid` nor `pending`); `aria-roledescription=
  "draggable bid"` when draggable (NO-PORT with the drag). Head
  (`qt-storyteller-card-head`): the order badge `<span class="qt-storyteller-
  card-order" title={`Speaks ${ordinal(n)}`}>{n}</span>` when `arranged &&
  selected` (`n` = 1-based position among `chosenBids`; `ordinal`: `first …
  tenth`, else `#${n}` — `:152-155`); the checkbox (only `status === 'bid'`)
  `aria-label={`Let ${bid.characterName} go ahead`}` `disabled`; `<Avatar
  name={characterName} src={avatarFor(participantId) ?? null} size="xs"/>`;
  `<span class="qt-storyteller-card-name" title={characterName}>`; the tier
  badge (`bid` & `tier`) `qt-badge ${TIER_BADGE[tier]}` with `TIER_BADGE =
  {simple: 'qt-badge-secondary', lookup: 'qt-badge-info', act:
  'qt-badge-warning', destroy: 'qt-badge-destructive'}` (`:71-76`) and text
  `TIER_LABELS[tier]`. Then: `bid.plan` in `qt-storyteller-plan` (bid only);
  the status line in `qt-storyteller-meta` (`statusLine`, `:89-102`:
  `pending` → `Considering…`; `pass` → `plan ? `` `Passes: ${plan}` `` :
  `` `Passes${reason ? ` — ${reason}` : ''}` ``; `error` → `` `No bid — ${reason
  ?? 'the call failed'}` ``; `kept-quiet` → `Kept quiet this round`; `bid` →
  null); the Destroy warning `<div class="qt-storyteller-warning">This bid
  means to delete something, and cannot be undone. It goes ahead alone.</div>`
  (bid & destroy); the raised line (bid & `tierRaised` & `statedTier` &
  `tier`) `Asked for {TIER_LABELS[statedTier]}, named {tier === 'act' ? 'an' :
  'a'} {TIER_LABELS[tier]} tool.`; `Tools: {tools.join(', ')}` (bid &
  non-empty); `{reason}` (bid & reason); ALWAYS `Talkativeness
  {talkativeness.toFixed(2)}{silent ? ' · silent' : ''}`; `Ask anyway`
  (`qt-button-secondary qt-button-sm`, `kept-quiet` or `error`, `disabled ||
  ask.isPending`).
- **Below the cards** (`:387-427`): when `biddable.length > 1`, ONE
  `qt-storyteller-meta` line: `arranged ? 'Chosen bids run in the order
  shown.' : 'Drag the cards to set the running order; left alone, the room’s
  rotation decides.'` (U+2019 in `room’s`); `destroyMixed` → `<div
  class="qt-storyteller-warning">A Destroy bid must be chosen on its own.
  </div>`; the actions row `qt-storyteller-actions`: `Run in order`
  (`qt-button-primary qt-button-sm`, `disabled || chosenBids.length === 0 ||
  destroyMixed`), `All at once` (`qt-button-secondary qt-button-sm`,
  `disabled || chosenBids.length < 2 || destroyMixed`, title `Simple and
  Lookup bids speak at once; Act and Destroy bids follow one at a time`),
  `Let it lie` (`qt-button-ghost qt-button-sm`, `disabled || lie.isPending`).
- **The pure helpers:** `arrangeBids(bids, arrangement)` (`:108-117`): null →
  `bids`; else the named ids in arrangement order (unknown ids skipped), then
  every bid not named, in round order. `withBid(round, bid)` (`:158-163`):
  replace the card with the same `participantId`, else append.
- **The drag** (`:17-35` imports; `:172-177` sensors: `MouseSensor
  {distance: 8}`, `TouchSensor {delay: 250, tolerance: 5}`, `KeyboardSensor
  sortableKeyboardCoordinates`; `:229-237` `handleDragEnd` → `arrayMove`;
  `:127-150` `SortableCard`, only `status === 'bid' && !disabled` draggable)
  — NO-PORT under R-L; Tier 2's arrangement reaches the same `arrangement`
  state by keyboard + buttons (R-1).
- **v4's ONE client test:** `__tests__/unit/components/chat/storyteller-board-
  arrange.test.ts` — four `it`s over `arrangeBids`: `keeps the round order
  when nothing has been arranged` (`null` → `['a','b','c']`), `follows the
  operator arrangement` (`['c','a','b']`), `appends a late bid the arrangement
  does not name` (`[a,b,c,d]` with `['b','a','c']` → `['b','a','c','d']`),
  `drops an arranged seat that is no longer in the round` (`['x','c','b','a']`
  → `['c','b','a']`). Transcribe name for name.

### v4 at the pin — the sidebar control + the hooks

- **`components/chat/ChatSidebar.tsx`**: props `turnMode?: 'rotation' |
  'storyteller'` (`:156`), `onSetTurnPosition?: (position: 'normal' |
  'paused' | 'storyteller') => void` (`:158`), `storytellerInviteFilter?:
  boolean` (`:160`), `onToggleStorytellerInviteFilter?: () => void` (`:161`).
  `TURN_POSITIONS` (`:770-774`): `{id:'normal', label:'Normal', title:'The
  company takes turns on its own'}`, `{id:'paused', label:'Paused',
  title:'Nobody answers until you nudge them'}`, `{id:'storyteller',
  label:'Storyteller', title:'Each of your posts asks the company who wishes
  to answer; you choose'}`. `TurnModeControl` (`:776-808`): `current =
  isPaused ? 'paused' : turnMode === 'storyteller' ? 'storyteller' : 'normal'`;
  `<div class="qt-storyteller-mode mt-3" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Turn
  mode">` of three `<button type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={active}
  class="qt-storyteller-mode-option[ qt-storyteller-mode-option-paused |
  qt-storyteller-mode-option-active]" title={title} onClick={() => { if
  (!active) onSet(id) }}><span>{label}</span></button>` — the active PAUSED
  option wears `-paused`, any other active one `-active`; clicking the active
  one does nothing. Mounted at `:852-858` (`isPaused={p.isPaused === true}`,
  `turnMode={p.turnMode ?? 'rotation'}`). The invite checkbox (`:860-873`),
  rendered only when `onSetTurnPosition && turnMode === 'storyteller' &&
  onToggleStorytellerInviteFilter`: `<label class="flex items-center gap-2
  cursor-pointer mt-2" title="Each round, a quiet character may be left out —
  the likelier the less talkative. Kept-quiet seats can still be asked from
  the board."><input type="checkbox" checked={storytellerInviteFilter ===
  true} onChange={onToggle} class="qt-checkbox"/><span class="qt-label">
  Invite by talkativeness</span></label>`. The old Pause button (`:875-884`)
  renders only when `onTogglePause && !onSetTurnPosition`; the COLLAPSED
  strip's pause button (`:685-697`, `qt-chat-sidebar-collapsed-pause`) is
  unchanged — v4 keeps it.
- **`app/salon/[id]/hooks/useChatControls.ts:226-262`**: `setTurnPosition
  (position)` — `'paused'` → `await setPauseState(true)` + toast
  `Auto-responses paused` + return; else `turnMode = position ===
  'storyteller' ? 'storyteller' : 'rotation'`; optimistic `isPaused = false`
  + `chat = {...prev, isPaused: false, turnMode}`; `PUT /api/v1/chats/${id}`
  body `{ chat: { isPaused: false }, turnMode }` (the comment: "turnMode rides
  beside `chat`, not in it: it is patch-only on the row and has its own setter
  server-side"); success toast `turnMode === 'storyteller' ? 'Storyteller
  mode: each of your posts asks the company who wishes to answer' :
  'Auto-responses resumed'`; `catch` → error toast `Could not change the turn
  mode` + `console.error('[useChatControls] turn mode change failed', error)`;
  ALWAYS `await fetchChat()`. `toggleStorytellerInviteFilter` — `next =
  !(chat?.storytellerInviteFilter === true)`; optimistic set;
  `persistChatField(chatId, { storytellerInviteFilter: next }, 'storyteller
  invite filter', rollback + error toast 'Could not change the invitation
  setting')`.
- **`app/salon/[id]/hooks/useSSEStreaming.ts`**: `StorytellerStreamEvent`
  (`:17-20`: `{kind:'round'; round}` | `{kind:'bid'; roundId; bid}` |
  `{kind:'open'; roundId}`); `ConcurrentStream {participantId; content}`
  (`:23-26`); the frame type gains `concurrent?`, `storytellerRound?`,
  `storytellerBid?: {roundId; bid}`, `roundOpen?`, `roundId?` (`:155-166`);
  the reader (`:705-717`) hands the three board frames to
  `onStorytellerEvent` and RETURNS (nothing else folds from them); a
  `concurrent && participantId` frame (`:722-760`) keeps one bubble per
  participant — `turnStart` adds it (and `setWaitingForResponse(false)`),
  `content` appends (creating the bubble if absent), `done` removes it and,
  when `messageId && !skipped && !stopped`, pushes an ASSISTANT message `{id:
  messageId, role:'ASSISTANT', content: finished, createdAt: now,
  participantId: pid, provider: data.provider || null, modelName: data.
  modelName || null, routeTrail: data.routeTrail ?? null, isSilentMessage:
  data.isSilentMessage || undefined, reasoningContent: data.reasoningContent ??
  null, reasoningSegments: data.reasoningSegments ?? null}` (deduped by id);
  `carinaAnswer` / `hostAnnouncement` / `pascalResult` surfaced; `error`
  toasted. `runStorytellerSelection(roundId, participantIds, order, keepOrder
  = false): Promise<boolean>` (`:1543-1662`): streaming or waiting → info
  toast `One moment — the room is still speaking.` + `false`; aborts any
  open controller; resets (`waitingForResponse = true`, `streaming = false`,
  content / tool batches / concurrent streams cleared); POST
  `?action=storyteller-run` `{roundId, participantIds, order, keepOrder}`;
  `!res.ok` → `Error(errorData.error || 'The storyteller could not run that
  selection')`; reads the stream in the chain dialect (`onTurnStart` sets the
  responding participant + resets the bubble + `waitingForResponse = true`;
  `onDone` / `onIntermediateDone` → `pushReply` — `skipped || stopped` →
  `finishSkippedTurn()`, blank → nothing, else push the ASSISTANT message with
  the same fields, `id: data.messageId || `` `storyteller-${Date.now()}` ``,
  `participantId: data.participantId || currentParticipantId`;
  `onTurnComplete` → reset + `fetchChat()`; `onChainComplete` → reset,
  `concurrentStreams = []`, `respondingParticipantId = null`,
  `scrollOnStreamComplete()`, `fetchChat()`, `announceChainPause(event)`,
  `focusInput()`); `catch` → non-abort → error toast
  `extractErrorMessage(err)`, `false`; `finally` resets everything incl.
  `setHandRaised(false)`, `stoppingRef = false`, `setStopping(false)`, one
  more `fetchChat()`, `notifyQueueChange()`, `focusInput()`. Returns
  `runStorytellerSelection`, `concurrentStreams` (`:1768-1769`).
- **`app/salon/[id]/SalonView.tsx`**: `handleStorytellerEvent` (`:558-570`):
  `round` → `setQueryData(key, { round: event.round })`; `bid` → `setQueryData

  waitingForResponse || sending} avatarFor={pid => participants.find(p =>

- **`VirtualizedMessageList.tsx:132-136, :421-437`**: one `<StreamingMessage>`

### The wire you consume — contract W1 (BINDING names; copied from the server order; stub THESE in the specs)

TS discriminants are the serde names of P4.D343's variants (the P4.D336
precedent: `Request::ChatRaiseHand` ↔ `type: 'chatRaiseHand'`; `Response::
RaiseHand` ↔ `{ type: 'raiseHand'; data }`).

- **`ChatUpdateRequest.turnMode?: 'rotation' | 'storyteller'`** — a SIBLING of
  `chat` (serde `turnMode`, `double_option`): absent → untouched;
  `"rotation"` / `"storyteller"` → the server's `set_turn_mode` (+ the round
  abandoned on leaving storyteller mode); `null` or any other value → the
  dispatch REJECTS with `BadRequest` `Validation error` and NOTHING is written
  (the bag included). The bag key `chat.storytellerInviteFilter: boolean`
  (a non-boolean refuses the whole PUT). The SPA's turn-mode body is EXACTLY
  `{ type: 'chatUpdate', chatId, chat: { isPaused: false }, turnMode }`.
- **`ChatStorytellerRoundRequest { type: 'chatStorytellerRound'; chatId }`** →
  `{ type: 'storytellerRound'; data: { round: StorytellerRound | null } }`.
- **`ChatStorytellerRunRequest { type: 'chatStorytellerRun'; chatId; roundId:
  string; participantIds: string[]; order?: StorytellerOrder; keepOrder?:
  boolean }`** → `{ type: 'storytellerRun'; data: null }` — the reply is
  EMPTY; every frame rides the chat-scoped `Event` channel exactly like
  `chatSend`'s. Refusals BEFORE any frame (the dispatch rejects): `Conflict`
  409 `That round is no longer open`; `NotFound` 404 `Chat not found`;
  `BadRequest` 400 `Choose at least one bid, or let the round lie` / `Only a
  seat that bid to speak can be chosen` / `A Destroy bid must be chosen on its
  own`; `BadRequest` `Invalid request` for an `order` outside `sequential |
  simultaneous`; **until P4.D343's Tier 2 lands `simultaneous`:**
  `Unprocessable` `not_available: storyteller simultaneous` for `order:
  "simultaneous"` (AFTER the 400 / 404 / 409 gates).
- **`ChatStorytellerAskRequest { type: 'chatStorytellerAsk'; chatId; roundId;
  participantId }`** → `{ type: 'storytellerAsk'; data: { bid: StorytellerBid
  } }` (the ANSWERED card — the dispatch awaits the bid call); refusals 409
  `That round is no longer open`, 404 `That seat is not on this round`.
- **`ChatStorytellerLieRequest { type: 'chatStorytellerLie'; chatId; roundId
  }`** → `{ type: 'storytellerLie'; data: { closed: true } }` (409 as above).
- **Frames on the chat `Event` channel** (`ChatStreamFrame` keys):
  `{ storytellerRound: StorytellerRound }`; `{ storytellerBid: { roundId:
  string; bid: StorytellerBid } }`; `{ roundOpen: true, roundId: string }`;
  `{ chainComplete: true, reason: 'round_open', nextSpeakerId: null,
  chainDepth: 0 }` — in THAT order on a storyteller send, after the ordinary
  `status initializing` → `turnStart` → `status resolving` → the
  post-recording frames (a held post still NAMES the seat that would have
  answered). On a run: per sequential turn `turnStart { participantId,
  characterName, chainDepth ≥ 1 }` → the turn's own frames (`status`,
  `content` …, `done`) → `turnComplete { participantId, messageId, chainDepth,
  skipped? }`; after all, ONE `chainComplete` with `reason` `user_turn |
  yielded | stopped` (`stopped` carries `paused: true`, `chainDepth:
  turns.length`). A concurrent turn's EVERY frame carries `participantId`
  (added only when the frame lacked one) and `concurrent: true` as the LAST
  two keys (Tier 2).
- **TS types** (field for field from v4 `lib/schemas/storyteller.types.ts`,
  optional / nullable preserved): `StorytellerTier = 'simple' | 'lookup' |
  'act' | 'destroy'`; `StorytellerOrder = 'sequential' | 'simultaneous'`;
  `StorytellerBidStatus = 'pending' | 'bid' | 'pass' | 'error' | 'kept-quiet'`;
  `StorytellerBid { participantId: string; characterId: string;
  characterName: string; status: StorytellerBidStatus; statedTier?:
  StorytellerTier | null; tier?: StorytellerTier | null; tierRaised: boolean;
  tools: string[]; plan?: string | null; reason?: string | null;
  talkativeness: number; silent: boolean; askedAt?: string | null;
  answeredAt?: string | null }`; `StorytellerSelection { participantIds:
  string[]; order: StorytellerOrder | null; chosenAt: string; names: string[]
  }`; `StorytellerRound { id: string; chatId: string; triggerMessageId:
  string | null; status: 'open' | 'running' | 'closed'; bids:
  StorytellerBid[]; selection?: StorytellerSelection | null; closedReason?:
  'ran' | 'let-lie' | 'superseded' | 'abandoned' | null; createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string; closedAt?: string | null }`; `StorytellerBidRecord {
  roundId: string; tier: StorytellerTier; plan: string; toolsNamed: string[]
  }`; `TIER_LABELS: Record<StorytellerTier, string> = { simple: 'Simple',
  lookup: 'Lookup', act: 'Act', destroy: 'Destroy' }`.
- **The chat GET wire** (`chatGet` → `ChatDetail`): `turnMode: 'rotation' |
  'storyteller'` (NEVER null), `storytellerInviteFilter: boolean`, both right
  after `isPaused`. **Messages:** `MessageDto.storytellerBid?:
  StorytellerBidRecord | null` before `pascalMeta` (`core-contract.ts:3306`).
  **Logs:** `LlmLogDto.storytellerRoundId?: string | null` beside
  `autonomousRunId`; `LlmLogType` gains `'STORYTELLER_BID'` LAST. **Workbench:**
  `CustomToolLibraryEntry.effect: 'read' | 'write'` after `triggerCount`
  (`intervention` sits between them once P4.D345 / P4.D346 land —
  `LibraryEntryKeys`). **Realtime:** topic `"storytellerRounds"` scoped by
  chat id, the NINTH (after `inventory`; P4.D346's `customToolInterventions`
  TENTH) → the SPA invalidates `chatKeys.storytellerRound(chatId)`.

**On main's server today** the four storyteller dispatches answer the
`unknown variant` refusal (the `chatStopTurn` precedent before P4.D335 landed
— P4.D336's beat (F)); `chatGet` carries no `turnMode` key (→ `'rotation'`
through `turnModeOf`); and `chatUpdate` with a `turnMode` SIBLING is
**silently accepted** — `Request` carries no `deny_unknown_fields`
(`crates/quilltap-core/src/api/types.rs:383-405`: `concierge_state` is the
only typed sibling; an unknown key is dropped by serde), so the bag
`{ isPaused: false }` is written, the dispatch SUCCEEDS, the success toast
shows, and the refetched chat still has no `turnMode` → the radiogroup
snaps back to `Normal` and the board never mounts. That degradation (no
error, no board, the radiogroup reverting) is the ungated e2e beat (F)'s
premise — MEASURE it with one `dispatchRaw` against main's server at lane
start and record the bytes; it is what the unifier's flip must not regress
(after the flip, `turnMode: 'storyteller'` persists and (A) supersedes (F)).

## Tiered deliverables

### Tier 1 — must land

1. **Red-first, on main's tree in the worktree, BEFORE any source edit:** (a)
   `grep -rn 'storyteller\|turnMode\|STORYTELLER_BID' apps/web/src apps/web/e2e`
   → 0 (record); (b) `npm run lint` + `npx vitest run` whole-suite counts as
   the baseline (planning: 568 files / 13,121 tests — WCAG survey header; take
   your own); (c) the live measurement of the three degradations against
   main's server (the W1 section's last paragraph): `dispatchRaw({type:
   'chatStorytellerRound', chatId})` → the `unknown variant` bytes;
   `dispatchRaw({type:'chatUpdate', chatId, chat:{isPaused:false},
   turnMode:'storyteller'})` → success + the refetched chat lacking `turnMode`;
   `chatGet` → no `turnMode` / `storytellerInviteFilter` keys — record all
   three verbatim in the lane record; (d) for every spec you write against an
   unported file, count the red per arm with a scratch shim (memory note
   `spa-component-harness-gotchas`: whole-tree type-check — a spec importing a
   not-yet-existing export fails the build; add a passthrough shim, run,
   remove, never commit). **The stand-in rule (R-V of the previous round,
   carried):** `const P4D343_SERVER_LANDED = false;` in EVERY spec file that
   has a beat only a landed server satisfies (the e2e file; the component
   specs need none — they stub the dispatch); gated cases `it.skipIf
   (!P4D343_SERVER_LANDED, GATE_REASON)` / `test.skip(!P4D343_SERVER_LANDED,
   GATE_REASON)` with an ungated twin for your half; no throwaway stand-in
   TYPE is expected to be needed (W1 gives every TS shape; you declare them
   yourself in `core-contract.ts`).

### Tier 2 — should land (separate commits, after Tier 1 is green)

15. **Arrangement WITHOUT drag (R-1 — ruling R-L applied):** the board gains
    an `arrangement = signal<string[] | null>(null)` reset with the selection
    on a new round id; `cards = computed(() => arrangeBids(round().bids,
    arrangement()))`; `arranged = computed(() => arrangement() !== null)`;
    the mechanism is keyboard + buttons with NO dependency: each `status ===
    'bid'` card (when not `disabled`) carries two icon buttons in its head
    `aria-label="Move <name> earlier"` / `aria-label="Move <name> later"`
    (R3 — v5 strings; v4 has none for this control) and the card itself
    `tabindex="0"` with `ArrowUp` / `ArrowLeft` → earlier, `ArrowDown` /
    `ArrowRight` → later (the `KeyboardSensor` twin), each move = v4's
    `arrayMove(ids, from, to)` over `cards.map(id)` → `arrangement.set(...)`
    (`:229-237`); `aria-roledescription` is NOT emitted (no drag);
    `cardClass`'s `-draggable` / `-dragging` tokens are NEVER emitted
    (recorded). Once arranged: the order badge `<span class="qt-storyteller-
    card-order" [title]="'Speaks ' + ordinal(n)">{{ n }}</span>` on each
    SELECTED card (n = its 1-based position among `chosenBids`), `Reset order`
    in the board tools (title `Put the cards back and let the room's rotation
    set the order`) → `arrangement.set(null)`, the hint line `Chosen bids run
    in the order shown.`, and `run` emits `keepOrder: true` with
    `participantIds` in BOARD order. Unarranged with `biddable.length > 1`,
    the hint is v5's own sentence (v4's promises dragging):
    `Use a card’s arrows to set the running order; left alone, the room’s
    rotation decides.` (U+2019 twice) — a NAMED both-ways divergence
    `BOARD_ARRANGE_HINT_WITHOUT_DRAG` in the lane record: `(v4: 'Drag the
    cards to set the running order; left alone, the room’s rotation decides.'
    + three dnd-kit sensors; v5: the arrows sentence + keyboard / button
    moves)`; the arranged sentence and every other byte identical. ⚖ The
    human may re-word the v5 sentence (voice) — flag it in the record. Spec:
    the four `arrangeBids` cases drive the component (move `c` first → the
    badges read `1`/`2` in the new order, `Reset order` appears, `run` emits
    `keepOrder: true` in that order; a late bid lands LAST; a seat gone from
    the round is dropped); the keyboard twin; a non-bid card has no arrows.

## The differential requirement, restated

This lane ships no Rust and owns no oracle family; its equivalence evidence
is the SPA tier's: v4's ONE client test transcribed NAME FOR NAME
(`__tests__/unit/components/chat/storyteller-board-arrange.test.ts`'s four
`arrangeBids` cases → `chat/storyteller-board.pure.spec.ts`) and, for every
surface v4 shipped WITHOUT a test (the board, the radiogroup, the hooks, the
frame reader, the Inspector — survey §5.6 lists only the arrange test on the
client), v5-authored specs written FROM THE HUNKS with v4's strings
byte-for-byte: the five summary texts, the six status lines, the Destroy
warning and the raised line (`an` / `a`), `Tools: `, `Talkativeness 0.50 ·
silent`, the three enablement predicates and the `All at once` title, the
destroy-exclusive toggle rule and `Select all`'s exclusion, the selection
reset on a new round id, the three positions' labels / titles / classes and
the no-op click on the active one, the invite checkbox's label + title and
its storyteller-only render, the four toasts (`Storyteller mode: each of your
posts asks the company who wishes to answer` / `Auto-responses resumed` /
`Auto-responses paused` / `Could not change the turn mode`) + the
`console.error` line + `Could not change the invitation setting` + `Could not
ask for that bid` + `Could not close the round` + `One moment — the room is
still speaking.` + `The storyteller could not run that selection`, the
dispatch bodies (`turnMode` a SIBLING of `chat`; `keepOrder` always a
boolean), the three carried frames + `round_open`, the cache writes (`round`
replaces, `bid` patches ONLY the matching round through `getQueryData`,
`open` invalidates), the chain-dialect fold of a run, the Inspector's
admission rule and chip. The server half is proven by its owner: P4.D343's
`chat_events` self-tests pin the frame bytes you stub, its
`chat_turn_mode_routes_equivalence` the PUT sibling + the four verbs' bodies
and refusal texts, its `storyteller_round_tier3` the frame ORDER of a send
and a run, its `realtime_topics_equivalence` the ninth topic — and the
unifier flips your gated beats against the real server at §S.9, which is the
one LIVE proof that the stub shapes matched. No sweep, no recipe header, no
`/tmp/qt-*` fixture path; no `npx jest` / `npx tsx` run against the pin; no
IMPORT-DEAD re-aim is this lane's (the storyteller survey §5.3: the one dead
case, `tool-build.test.ts`, is P4.D343's).

## Rulings made at planning (the planner's calls; the human may overrule before lane start — a STOP item if they do)

Items the HUMAN must rule on first:

- ⚖ **THE NPM CACHE GAP** (the preamble) — binds every lane that runs an
  oracle; this lane runs none. If the human's ruling changes the round's pin
  recipe, nothing here moves.
- ⚖ **R-L (round-wide) — the board ships WITHOUT drag; no `@dnd-kit`, no
  `@angular/cdk`.** The alternative (add `@angular/cdk` for `DragDrop`) is a
  dependency addition — a ruling by standing rule, and refused by the round.
  If the human overrules and ADMITS a dependency, Tier 2 item 15 becomes a
  `cdkDropList` port of v4's sensors (mouse distance 8 / touch delay 250 /
  keyboard) with v4's `Drag the cards…` sentence restored and
  `BOARD_ARRANGE_HINT_WITHOUT_DRAG` retired — say which in the lane record.
- ⚖ **The v5 hint sentence of item 15** (`Use a card’s arrows to set the
  running order; left alone, the room’s rotation decides.`) is a v5-authored
  user-facing string (v4's promises dragging) — the human may re-word it;
  the lane ships it as written and flags it.

The planner's calls for this lane:

- **R-1 — arrangement by keyboard + per-card buttons, Tier 2, NO
  dependency; Tier 1 ships the board unarranged with the hint OMITTED.**
  Why: v4's `arrangement` state and everything visible about it (the order
  badges, `Reset order`, `Chosen bids run in the order shown.`, `keepOrder:
  true` in BOARD order) are reachable without drag; only the gesture differs.
  Alternative: a hand-rolled pointer sortable (survey §6.1 option (b)) — a
  pointer-events state machine with no test oracle and a long tail of touch
  edge cases; refused for this round (a later round may add it under the same
  `arrangement` state). The both-ways table `BOARD_ARRANGE_HINT_WITHOUT_DRAG`
  records the divergence.
- **R-2 — the e2e mock is SPEC-LOCAL and BID-AWARE; `e2e/support/mock-llm.ts`
  is not edited.** v4's bid call and speaking turn hit the SAME provider; a
  fixed reply would make every speaking turn "say" the four bid lines (the
  survey's fallback) — acceptable for a board assertion but it hides a
  mis-routed call (a speaking turn answered with a bid, or a bid answered with
  prose, both pass). Keying the reply on the bid section's bytes (`[Turn note
  from the Salon — not spoken by any character]` + `answer only with your
  bid`) and on the plan section's first line (`The storyteller chose you to
  go ahead`) proves the SERVER sent the right prompt to the right call — a
  free tier-3-grade check inside the e2e. Alternative: extend
  `startMockLlm` with a `replyFor(body)` option — a shared file edit (the
  previous round's §R.10 (z): "each lane its NEW spec file only") — refused.
- **R-3 — the round query lives in the BOARD component** (`injectQuery` on
  `chatKeys.storytellerRound(chatId)`, `refetchInterval: realtime.
  refetchInterval(60_000)`), the cache WRITES live in the Salon vertical's
  `reportStreamTransitions` (the `handAnswered` precedent), and the fold is
  the reducer's CARRIED slot (`storyteller` + `storytellerSeq`). Why: v4's
  split is the same (the board queries; `SalonView.handleStorytellerEvent`
  writes; the hook hands the frames up) and the reducer must stay pure.
  Alternative: the board subscribing to the `events$` stream itself —
  refused (two readers of one stream; the Salon already folds it).
- **R-4 — `turnMode` is read through ONE `turnModeOf(chat)` helper** (`??
  'rotation'`), never a scattered default: the wire never sends null (W1),
  an older server omits the key, and the e2e's ungated beat (I) depends on
  the absent key reading as rotation. The TS field is optional for that
  reason alone; the server's type is non-optional.
- **R-5 — the board's `running` state is the Salon's `busy()`**: v4 disables
  the board's buttons for `busy || running`, and in v5 `busy()` (`stream() !=
  null`) is true for the whole run the board emitted; a second local flag
  would be a second source of truth (the P4.D336 R-2 lesson). The `run`
  output is fire-and-forget from the board's side; the Salon owns the
  promise. Alternative: the board awaiting a callback input — refused
  (Angular outputs do not return promises; a `runDone` input is a signal
  round-trip for no observable gain).
- **R-6 — the round key sits under the `detail` prefix** (item 22) — the
  `informs` / `gallery` precedent; recorded as a harmless superset.
- **R-7 — `salon-conversation.ts` ownership = ANCHORS, not applications,
  for hunks that need a sibling's file** (H3's announcer, H4, H5); the `<h1>`
  (no dependency) this lane applies itself. Why: binding a sibling's
  not-yet-landed component or import fails Angular's strict templates
  (memory note `react-closure-reads-in-signal-ports`); a fenced anchor is a
  byte-stable drop point the unifier can find. Alternative: throwaway
  stand-in components measured once — refused (three stand-ins for three
  siblings is more machinery than the hunks).
- **R-8 — the five e2e Pause sites move in THIS lane** (item 12): the
  button they select is gone on your branch, so those files are red on your
  branch today — they cannot wait for the unifier. Two of the four files
  overlap P4.D358's toast-line rewrite on DIFFERENT lines (H11); cherry-pick
  its first commit when it exists.
- **R-9 — "resume" in the e2e helpers clicks `Normal`**, not a `Resume`
  button: v4's `setTurnPosition('normal')` writes `{ chat: { isPaused:
  false }, turnMode: 'rotation' }` — on a rotation chat the same net state
  the old Resume produced (and on main's server today the `turnMode` key is
  dropped, so the body degrades to the old `chat: { isPaused: false }`).
  The `Auto-responses resumed` toast is unchanged.
- **R-10 — the effect badge is P4.D346's to place, the type line yours**
  (correction 2); the logs-card label is P4.D358's (correction 3). One owner
  per file (round ruling R-J); the fallback ("the unifier applies from the
  record") keeps the hunk from being lost if the owner declines.
- **R-11 — the concurrent bubbles are Tier 2** with the server's
  `simultaneous`; the `All at once` button keeps v4's enablement in Tier 1 and
  the refusal text toasts through v4's own error path — no capability gate,
  no v5-only disablement (a disabled button would be a v5 divergence v4 never
  shows).
- **R-12 — `host: { class: 'block' }` on the board component**, not a
  `qt-*` host class: the census polices host classes (`check-qt-classes.mjs
  :160-166`), and `_storyteller.css` styles the inner `<section>` /
  `<div>`s, never a host.
- **R-13 — the Playwright file is ONE file, own server 4353, mock 45351**
  (the planner's port table; no existing spec uses either — measured
  `grep -rln '4353\|45351' e2e/ src/` → none).

from `main` at the orders' commit and start at once (P4.D346's grammar-twin
part and P4.D359's chips may stack on a sibling's Tier-1 commit — §R.14).
**No REAIM lane:** the 2026-10-29 import-dead census at the pin found eight
dead leaf oracle sites (seven import-dead leaf cases + one `require.resolve`)
and NO fixture builder hit (§R.4 (a)); each case is re-aimed by the lane that
owns its family.

The survey of record for every order is ITS "Survey-verified starting
points" section PLUS the named planning survey(s) under
`work-orders/surveys/2026-10-29-setupphase-*.md` (fresh 2026-10-29 at
`236c04e35`, v5 `main` `f6cdceedd`) — the long form, read WHOLE before the
first edit. The unifier cherry-picks in §S.1's order, gates ONCE from a
`236c04e35` pin, and `merge --ff-only`s. **The oracle baseline MOVES to
`236c04e35` at the unification** — every §3 row absorbed or ratified (§S.6);
any v4 commit the lanes observe past the pin becomes a new UNPROCESSED row
(`8832cfd20` and `bf7a457f6` already are).

- **§R.1 — Baseline, target, and the PIN RULE (the human's ruling,

  236c04e35 -- lib app packages plugins help components` is EMPTY) — so

  show 236c04e35:<path>` for a pin file, `git show <sha> -- <path>` for one
  commit's hunk, `show 322a5488e:<path>` for the PRE shape v5 ported, `git
  diff 322a5488e 236c04e35 -- <paths>` for the span. Never the commit
  message, never an order's prose, **never the working tree** (it is past

- **§R.2 — The freshness probe is an OBSERVATION, not a gate (the pin
  rule).** Run the drift ledger's §2 probe at lane start and record its
  output. `log 236c04e35..main` is NOT empty today (`8832cfd20`,
  `bf7a457f6`, and whatever lands later) and the tree is DIRTY (the
  chokidar removal in `lib/file-storage/watcher.ts` / `lib/mount-index/
  watcher.ts` / `package.json`) — EXPECTED; RECORD ("v4 moved: `<sha>`
  observed at `<time>`; tree dirty: `<files>`") and CONTINUE — the pin
  worktree (§R.3) is unaffected. **STOP only when the PIN is unusable:**
  `git -C "$PIN" rev-parse HEAD` is not `236c04e35…`; or `git show
  236c04e35:package.json` does not read `4.12.0-dev.0`; or the pin's root
  `node_modules` cannot be made to match the pin's lockfile (§R.3's cure
  fails). A lane never writes the ledger, never re-pins, never ports a hunk
  newer than `236c04e35`.

  - (h) **The keystone-memory facts the row overstated:** `keystone` is a
    FLAG, not a tier (R-E) — `bucketCandidates` skips `memory.keystone`
    rows AFTER `rowsLoaded++`, BEFORE the `CONSOLIDATED` branch; `isDemotable
    = hot && AUTO && !keystone`; `policyProtectionOf` answers `'keystone'`
    FIRST and `sparedBy`'s key order becomes `{keystone, manual, digest,
    cold}`; a keystone fold survivor takes NO footnotes and an EMPTY patch
    but keeps links + the reinforcement delta; `cascadeEmptiedDigests` keeps
    a keystone digest whose last member retires (DEBUG `Keystone digest kept
    though its last member retires`) and its `added` now counts only rows
    actually doomed (`if (!grew) break`) — port as shipped; `protection()`
    answers `{score: 1, protected: true, protectedBySource: true}`. In
    recall, "every digest is cut before any keystone" holds only INSIDE the
    inner circle (`innerCircleTrimOrder` sorts `Number(b.keystone) −
    Number(a.keystone)` first): present-character keystones are LIFTED into
    the circle at `tier −1` ahead of that character's digests, self
    keystones join the anchors, and `renderArchive` / `renderHead` render a
    keystone's `content` first (`summary` first otherwise). `KEYSTONES_PER_
    SUBJECT_MAX = 5`; the v4 quirk — `POST /memories` with `keystone: true`
    answers 400 `The memory was saved, but not as a keystone: <msg>` AFTER
    writing the row — is ported as the bytes (Q3). `keystone` appears in
    EVERY parsed Memory (between `consolidatedAt` and `createdAt`), so every
    byte pin of a memory row moves; `hydrateRawRows` coerces number / bigint
    / null to a boolean and DROPS a string cell with the WARN.
  - (i) **The WCAG ownership rule** (wcag survey §7.3): "the lane with the
    larger or structural hunk owns the file; the a11y lane records a HANDOFF
    naming the rule(s) R1–R12 and v4's bytes; the owner applies them in its
    own commit; the unifier checks the handoff list. Ties go to the feature
    lane (the a11y edits are mechanical)." Every sibling SPA lane applies
    rules **R1–R12** (the binding text is Shared contract C9) to the files it
    owns and records each as a `HANDOFF:` for P4.D358's census; P4.D358's
    static census script takes an allowlist for sibling-owned files, which
    the unifier empties. Already CONVERGED (not reds): the sidebar switches,
    `Allow Any Character`, the edit box, the composer `Message`, the
    slide-over's focus. The real gap: of 34 overlay components, 13 have no
    `role="dialog"`; `qt-modal` (77 hosts) carries `role` + `aria-modal` +
    the close label but NO `aria-labelledby` / `tabindex` / focus / Escape.
  - (j) **`help/` + `docs/v4/` counts** (vendor survey §A / §B): `help/`
    135 → **137** at the pin (+`diagrams.md` 132 lines, +`storyteller-mode.md`
    120 lines; 35 pages modified; 37 files +662 / −78; NO existing `url:` /
    `title:` line moves; the two new `url:`s sort after existing matches so
    no resolved context changes — the Guide LIST order and `help_docs` rowid
    order DO); `custom-tools.md` carries FOUR commits' hunks, `salon-host-
    introductions.md` four, `memory-recall-relevance.md` three — per-lane
    patches refused by construction; re-vendor WHOLE. `docs/v4/`: 502 →
    **518** through **43 operations** — 18 overwrites, 19 adds (the 12
    fixed-bug files 258–269, `docker-test-plan.md`, four `complete/` specs,
    `composer-document-references.md`, `releases/4.11.0.md`), 3 deletes

    `while IFS= read -r` (one spaced filename lives under `docs/`); no

- **§R.5 — Log lines.** Every line this round ports or moves lands with a
  capture pin (the thread-scoped rig) AND its silence leg.
  - A line emitted INSIDE a `Db::write` closure runs on the writer thread and
    is INVISIBLE to the rig: run the home on the caller thread (memory note
    `capture-rig-writer-thread`) or use `global_capture`.
  - A `db::fallback` line renders the BARE SQLite / Zod message
    (`error_text`), never `sqlite error: …`.
  - A structured v4 field that is an array or object renders through the
    `…Json` file-layer convention; v4's camelCase keys are the keys; an
    `undefined` v4 field is OMITTED; child-logger bindings are fields on the
    line; `durationMs` and other wall-clock fields are MASKED.
  - Open before `captured_with`; a v4 catch UNREACHABLE through v4's real
    code is pinned NEGATIVE with a v5 fallback home.
  - **Migration lines follow the standing precedent** (P4.D249 / P4.D304 /
    P4.D315 / P4.D324, `host.rs`): v4's migration INFO / ERROR texts are
    NO-PORT and each boot ensure logs ONE v5-worded INFO per added column /
    table / index.

- **§R.7 — Red-first.**
  - **Measure red before touching source** — on unported `main` for the
    wave-0 lanes, on the `KEYSTONE` tip for its dependents (a contract
    refusal arm answering `not_available` IS the red). The lane record
    carries the red count per family. A test that passes before the fix is
    a test of nothing.
  - **Ruled divergences** are NAMED both-ways tables in the idiom of
    `FRESH_STORE_RESIDUAL` / `BOOT_CREATED_HISTORY_TABLE`, so a v4
    convergence trips them. This round's planned names: **P4.D345**
    `HOLD_KEEP_ALIVE_NO_STREAM` (v4's 15 s keep-alive ping — no v5
    counterpart by architecture), `ROUND_DROPS_MIN_EXCLUSIVE` (only if the
    corpus shows it); **P4.D354** `REFUSING_SEAM_LEAVES_PDF_UNEXTRACTED`
    (the backfill and the `_indexed` bridges leave a PDF / DOCX row at
    `'none'` while `DocumentTextExtractor::is_available()` is `false`; the
    REST path keeps v4's `failed` bytes); **P4.D347** `ECHO_SLICE_SURROGATE`
    + `SENTENCE_SLICE_SURROGATE` (a JS `slice` that cuts a surrogate pair —
    v5 rounds to the code-point boundary); **P4.D343** the dry scope's
    table-delta proof (whatever the `DryBuildContextSeams` wrapper does not
    cover is a recorded divergence, never a silent write) and `plugin_tool_
    effects` as NO-COUNTERPART; **P4.D350** `PUBLISH_AFTER_COMMIT` only if
    the human overrules its R-1 (v4 publishes at the write; v5 after the
    commit); **P4.D355** the explicit `terminal_sessions` DELETE on chat
    delete (v5's cascade stand-in on a generateDDL table with no FK — named
    in its record; R-R); **P4.D341** none owed (Q3's post-write 400 is
    ported as bytes); **P4.D352** an ADAPTER divergence recorded in the SPA
    (ProseMirror `Decoration.inline` for v4's CSS Custom Highlight API; the
    four `::highlight(name)` rules become class rules with the same tokens);
    **P4.D353** a Tier-3 code-block note only if R-B is overruled to (C).

- **§R.8 — The unifier's accumulation.** Each lane's record states its
  `Cargo.toml` versions at close; the unifier takes the HIGHEST and bumps +1
  for the union commit; `Cargo.lock` follows. The SPA: the highest of the
  SPA lanes, +1. CHANGELOG entries and `status-log.md` lane records
  union-merge (append-only).
- **§R.9 — One machine.**
  - **Workspace gates:** at most TWO Rust workspace gates at once. Count them
    with ONE `ps -ax -o pid,etime,command | grep "cargo test --workspace" |
    grep -v grep`; if two run, queue yours in ONE background command waiting
    on the siblings' PIDs (`while kill -0 <pidA> && kill -0 <pidB>; do sleep
    20; done; sleep $((RANDOM % 20)); <re-count once; if still two, wait on
    those PIDs>; cargo test …`), never on a pattern (memory notes
    `no-pattern-kill-on-shared-machine`, `parallel-lane-shared-resources`
    §2 — co-waiters all fire on one exit; re-count after the wait).
  - **Sweeps and Playwright:** one sweep at a time (announced); one
    Playwright run at a time (lanes by file only; the full suite is the
    unifier's). New own-instance e2e files' server ports are unique per
    lane (4319–4340, 4343–4350, 4352 and mocks 45301–45304, 45340–45348 are
    taken): **P4.D344 4353 / mock 45351**, **P4.D346 4354 / mock 45352**,
    **P4.D352 4355 / mock 45353**, **P4.D353 4356 / mock 45354**, **P4.D358
    4357 / mock 45355**, **P4.D359 4358 / mock 45356**, **P4.D355 4359 /
    mock 45357** (only if a terminal e2e is added). A lane that only edits
    an EXISTING spec takes no port.
  - **Long jobs:** `run_in_background: true`, full log in a lane-private
    file, awaited by the completion notification — NEVER a `pgrep`/`grep`
    poll loop (CLAUDE.md), never `tail -N` on a gate log. A gate reads its
    oracles and source at RUN time: no regen and no source edit while one
    runs (memory note `lane-gate-hygiene`).
  - **Targets:** clone main's `target/` before the first build (`cp -cR`;
    `/carryout` rule 8 — same volume only, memory note
    `apfs-clone-same-volume-only`); main's `target/` was warmed by the
    `/setupphase` chain on 2026-10-29 at the orders' commit (the test /
    clippy set the lanes clone; the release bins were swept — an
    own-instance e2e that prefers `target/release/*` must delete the stale
    release bins from its clone or build release, memory note
    `lane-spa-env-node-modules-and-release-bins`); a stacked lane may clone
    its parent lane's worktree target instead, when that lane is not
    mid-build.
  - **`apps/web/dist/`:** a fresh worktree has none — build it before any
    workspace clippy / test. **`apps/web/node_modules`:** `npm ci` in the
    lane (a cloned copy fails every vitest spec with NG0203).
  - **Concurrency:** the cap is the two-gate rule, not the lane count;
    twenty lanes do NOT all run at once (§S.0 waves). Disk at planning:
    144 GiB free on the repo volume with `target/` at 347 GB BEFORE the
    warm sweep — the human sizes the waves from the post-sweep numbers in
    the hand-off report.

- **§R.14 — Stacking rules.** Each named stacking commit — `KEYSTONE`
  (P4.D341), `CONTEXT` (P4.D347), `SPINE` (P4.D348), `CATALOG` (P4.D350's
  FIRST commit), `FILES` (P4.D354) — is ONE commit whose subject starts with
  its name; the owner records the sha in its status header ON ITS LANE
  BRANCH the moment it lands. A dependent lane — P4.D347 / P4.D342 / P4.D351
  / P4.D356 from `KEYSTONE`; P4.D348 from `CONTEXT`; P4.D343 / P4.D345 from
  `SPINE` (which is on `CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`); P4.D349 from `CATALOG`;
  P4.D355 from `FILES` — finds the sha per `/carryout` rule 2 (`git log --all
  --format='%h %s' | grep -E '^[0-9a-f]+ <NAME>:'`, confirmed against the
  parent's lane-branch copy of the order) and branches from it — never from
  `main`, never a guessed sha; if the parent has not recorded it, STOP and
  report. A stacked lane may PRE-START from `main` on work that calls none
  of its parent's contract names — P4.D342's prompt bytes (the four
  extraction ladders + the consolidation tail) + the inner-circle resolver
  (`services/inner_circle.rs`, the tuning knobs); P4.D343's `tools/effects.rs`
  + `pascal/tool_effect.rs` + `StreamParams.tool_choice` + the `tool-build`
  re-aim (its unit E) + the pure `storyteller/*` modules; P4.D345's hold
  registry + eligibility / overrides / recompute / spans + the engine's
  `ReplayInputs` + the ONE corpus regen + the schema re-vendor; P4.D351's
  `services/annotations.rs` + `template_prompt_hint.rs` ports; P4.D356's
  NanoGPT half + the Tier R verify; P4.D349's `pronoun-presets` /
  `pronoun-guidance` ports + the image-prompt `humanoid` threading; P4.D355's
  `db/log_entries.rs` + the terminal host; P4.D348's `operator_post.rs` /
  `whisper_send.rs` / `turn_control.rs` additions and the funnel recorder;
  P4.D347 has no pre-start (its first unit widens `RawMessage`, which rides
  the keystone's row shapes) — rebasing onto the parent's commit before the
  first commit that does call a contract name; that rebase is the one
  sanctioned exception to "no rebase mid-lane". Two sibling-stacked parts
  (not lane-named commits): P4.D346's grammar-twin part (`pascal/custom-tool-
  types.ts` + the corpus copy + the spec header) stacks on P4.D345's Tier-1
  commit, or authors blind and lets the unifier run `custom-tool-types.
  corpus.spec.ts` — the order states which; P4.D359's chips stack on
  P4.D353's Tier-1 commit (the `message-content.ts` hook). Every other lane
  cuts from `main` at the orders' commit.

## §S — Unifier wires (what no single lane can do)

- **§S.0 — Waves (the recommended execution; the two-gate cap rules the
  machine, not the lane count).**
  - **Wave 0, from the orders' commit, at once:** P4.D341 (→ `KEYSTONE`,
    the Opus / Fable tier), P4.D350 (→ `CATALOG` as its FIRST commit, then
    its own units), P4.D354 (→ `FILES`), and in parallel P4.D360, P4.D357,
    P4.D358. That is six worktrees.
  - **Wave 1, as slots free (still from the orders' commit):** the five SPA
    feature lanes P4.D344, P4.D346, P4.D352, P4.D353, P4.D359 (each authors
    against the contracts with gated beats; P4.D346's grammar-twin part and
    P4.D359's chips wait for their sibling's Tier-1 commit).
  - **Wave A, from `KEYSTONE`:** P4.D347 (→ `CONTEXT`) FIRST, then P4.D342,
    P4.D351, P4.D356 (its NanoGPT + Tier R half may have PRE-STARTED from
    `main`). **From `CATALOG`:** P4.D349 — FIRST among the independents
    (R-H). **From `FILES`:** P4.D355.
  - **Wave B, from `CONTEXT`:** P4.D348 (→ `SPINE`).
  - **Wave C, from `SPINE`:** P4.D343 and P4.D345 (the two turn-side
    consumers every later union depends on).

  Within a wave every lane compiles against the C sections below it alone.
- **§S.1 — The pick order.** P4.D341 → P4.D350 → P4.D349 → P4.D347 →
  P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 → P4.D342 → P4.D351 → P4.D356 → P4.D354 →
  P4.D355 → P4.D357 → P4.D344 → P4.D346 → P4.D352 → P4.D353 → P4.D359 →
  P4.D360 → P4.D358. The reasons per adjacency: `KEYSTONE` first (every
  server lane's row shapes, the dumps, the ensures); P4.D350 next because
  `CATALOG` is the base P4.D349 branches from, and P4.D349 right after it
  (R-H — the three LIVE-DATA erasure writers land before anything else
  touches a vault); P4.D347 → P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 in their stacking
  order (`CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`, `SPINE` on `CONTEXT`, the two consumers of
  S1 / S2 / C1 / `TurnStopReach` on `SPINE`; P4.D343 before P4.D345 so
  `trigger_runner.rs`'s pre-run ceiling block precedes the post-run `Vetoed`
  arm and `realtime/types.rs` lands `storytellerRounds` NINTH before
  `customToolInterventions` TENTH); P4.D342 and P4.D351 (keystone-only
  consumers, no spine seam) after the spine chain; P4.D356 after P4.D345 so
  the `customToolIntervention` arm it writes for P4.D345 and the
  `punctuationWarnings` key it writes for P4.D352 are in the tree when their
  families re-run, and after P4.D351 so `settings-routes` is regenerated
  ONCE at the end of the server picks; P4.D354 → P4.D355 (`FILES` then the
  two bridge sites switched); P4.D357 before every SPA lane (the CSS lines
  the fenced appends sit beside); P4.D344 before P4.D346 (`_index.css`
  imports in v4's order; `RealtimeTopicAppend`'s SPA half NINTH then TENTH;
  `LibraryEntryKeys`' SPA badges in v4's order); P4.D352 before P4.D353
  (`renderMarkdownToHtml` is P4.D352's; P4.D353 post-passes its HTML);
  P4.D353 before P4.D359 (P4.D359's chips stack on P4.D353's `message-
  content.ts` hook; its `_variables.css` append sits after P4.D353's);
  P4.D360 LATE (its help / docs guards judge a union carrying every server
  feature; its schema-guard CHECK sees the final constants; the P4.D339
  precedent: fourteenth) — nineteenth here, before P4.D358; P4.D358 LAST
  (its census allowlist for sibling-owned HANDOFF files is emptied against
  the finished union; its toast-selector first commit is cherry-picked
  EARLIER by any sibling that edits one of the 20 spec files).
- **§S.2 — The full sweep from the `236c04e35` pin** (`recipe_sweep.py
  --run-all --v4 /tmp/qt-v4-pin-unify-236c04e35 --v5w <the union worktree,

  every-boot seed that re-encodes the built-in templates' `narrationDelimiters`
  as JSON and inserts the Novel template with it `null`. **The first v5
  boot of a pre-`8cb584eff` Friday copy ADDS the eight columns + one table
  (column-gated, v4's shapes, every ensure UNSTAMPED) and REWRITES the
  built-in templates' delimiter cells `*` → `"*"`; a copy v4 migrated sees
  NO-OP ensures and a no-op seed (measure: the seed must be idempotent
  against v4's own output).** **The THREE vault-key ERASURE hazards RETIRE**
  once P4.D349 lands: `render_physical_prompts_json` carries `humanoid` (a
  v4 `"humanoid": false` survives a v5 physical write and the P4.82
  backfill respects it), `PropertiesJson` carries `canDrawDiagrams` /

  until that lane is on the union build, **no v5 write to a character v4
  edited past `6588972d5` / `b27e5089a` / `eb90d9ed8`** (reads stay safe).
  **The Log is carried** (`cc5f2347c`, P4.D355): a v5 backup writes
  `data/log-entries.json`, a `replace` restore / delete-all clears
  `log_entries`, a `new-account` restore remaps `id` / `chatId` /
  `characterId`; `.qtap` still omits it; a pre-round v5 backup has no Log
  file — restoring it on the union leaves the Log empty (expected).
  **Keystones protected:** maintenance, demotion, consolidation, fold and
  chat-wipe skip `keystone = 1` rows; recall lifts them into the circle; a
  v5 backup / `.qtap` carries the column; a pre-round v5 build must NOT
  run maintenance on a copy v4 keystoned. **Terminals:** kill sends SIGHUP
  then SIGKILL after 3 s; a deleted chat terminates its shells (and v5
  deletes its `terminal_sessions` rows explicitly); the daily sweep unlinks
  orphaned transcripts older than 1 h (`85c002822`, P4.D355). **Settings:**
  the chat-settings PUT refuses unknown keys with a 400 (`3b8b6f42b`) — a
  v5 SPA caller that echoed a GET row back now fails (P4.D356's census).

  uploads and a boot backfill fills old rows (`40fab1c54`, P4.D354 — a DATA
  WRITE on first boot of any copy with uploads: measure the row count; PDF
  / DOCX rows stay `'none'` under the refusing seam). **The general rule
  BITES: a v5 build from BEFORE this round must not open an instance this
  round's ensures have altered — the owed dogfood pass runs with the UNION
  build.** **NOT this round (`8832cfd20`, `bf7a457f6`):** v4 HEAD links
  SQLite3MC 2.4.0 / SQLite 3.53.4; v5 stays 2.3.5 / 3.53.2 — the first v5

- **§S.8 — The 💸 queue for the next `/dogfood`.** Each lane lists its own.
  The round's headline: **the first UNION boot of a Friday copy** (record
  the vintage FIRST — `PRAGMA table_info` for `memories` / `chats` /
  `chat_messages` / `chat_settings` / `roleplay_templates`, `sqlite_master`

  `@Name:`, whisper and nudge open no round; leaving the mode folds the
  board; a NanoGPT / DeepSeek bid request carries `"tool_choice":"none"`);
  💸 **an intervention hold on a real Pascal tool with a consult** (the
  dialog `Pascal holds: <title>`, `As rolled`, `Deal it` → the badge hover
  `Adjusted before it was dealt: …`, veto plain vs transparent texts, Stop
  while held (if `TurnStopReach` landed), reload reappears, two holds
  queued, Re-ask spending ONE `CUSTOM_TOOL_CONSULT` row, a hidden consult's
  compact section, the spans hints, the Automation toggle off, the 10-minute
  unattended hold over HTTP and Tauri, an autonomous room dealing as rolled);
  **a keystone memory surviving a consolidation + a backup / restore** (mark,
  `Keystones only`, a sixth refused with v4's sentence, a cold row refused;
  💸 recalled in FULL under `## Those Closest to You` / `## Memory Anchors`;
  💸 the inner circle's `tiers: 2` over a two-group holder; maintenance
  never folds / demotes it; `sparedBy` `{keystone, manual, digest, cold}`;
  a `.qtap` round trip and a `replace` restore keep it; `quilltap recall-
  replay --tuning '{"innerCircleShare":0.5,"innerCircleAllowance":[2]}'`;
  bash completion offers `keystone`); 💸 **a whisper that no longer chains**
  (one reply during a running chain, saved after it closes; one reply on an
  idle chat); 💸 **every Salon turn in the Inspector** (a stopped turn's row
  with partial content and no error; a provider error's row; a swipe's row
  under the new swipe's id; an `IMAGE_GENERATION` row from the Images route
  and in the Almanack's spend; the `Other` filter listing every unclaimed
  type); 💸 **the fair-rotation pause recording** a staged tool result, `roll
  2d6` and an image upload (TOOL rows, the rng row, the Librarian note); a
  paused chat with a keyless profile records the post; 💸 bug 259's whole
  attachment on every character's first turn; 💸 bug 263 / 264 / 265 row
  counts; 💸 **an absent character's name chip** (first mention, the hover
  card matching the ledger's card, `## Who is being spoken of` + `## The

  posting, a Continue Elsewhere notice with `off-scene-left-behind`; the
  dangling-pronoun line resolved); 💸 **a Novel-template chat** (narration
  italic unmarked, dialogue upright, `**` dropped, `((aside))` OOC, `* * *`
  a rule, multi-paragraph unclosed speech), the template editor copying
  Novel (`None — plain prose`, `Style unmarked text`, the asterisks box
  unticked), a `.qtap` export / import and a `replace` restore keeping
  `null` narration + `unmarkedStyle` + `markdownAsterisks: false`; **a

  💸 **a diagram fence rendered** (if R-B lands: Draws diagrams ON → the

  `canDrawDiagrams: true, diagramStyle: 'clean'` surviving a rename; a

  duplicate` with `{}` → 400; an operator REST create refreshing a second
  open dialog; **a backup carrying the Log** (`data/log-entries.json` with

  `terminal_sessions`); **a terminal kill that lands** (bash exits on
  SIGHUP; a `sleep 9999` shell → the SIGKILL WARN after 3 s; deleting a chat
  with a live Ariel → `[PTY] Terminated sessions of deleted chat` + the rows

  attached uploads; the first-boot backfill INFOs and `background_jobs`
  rows — 💸 if the embedding profile is remote; the Docker folder picker at

  `docker exec quilltap quilltap db --lock-status`; a corrupt sibling → a
  MOUNT_CHUNK job RETRIES; 💸 the wizard's PDF import still logs the new

  {"enabled":false}}` both transports; `{"foo":1}` → `Invalid settings:
  unknown key foo`; a planted `cheapLLMSettings = 5` → ONE row, the
  existing id with defaults + the WARN, the save writing the repair back;

  **NanoGPT image sizes fitted** (a portrait avatar on a tier model posts
  `size: 1k, aspect_ratio: 2:3`; a non-square turned down → the square retry

  queue badges; reduced-motion; VoiceOver: `Skip to content`, the Salon
  `<h1>`, 💸 one real turn announced `<name> replied: …`, five dialogs'

  Enter / Space on the clickable rows; 320 px toolbar wrap and 24 px tab

  137 rows on first boot with 35 `updatedAt` moves — 💸 the re-embed only if
  a profile is configured; the six theme previews showing the pin's tokens).
- **§S.9 — The gated beats flip LIVE.** P4.D344 / P4.D346 / P4.D352 /
  P4.D353 / P4.D359 author their Playwright beats behind gate constants
  (`const P4D343_SERVER_LANDED = false` etc.), with throwaway stand-ins
  deleted at the flip. On the union the unifier flips each to `true`,
  deletes the stand-ins, runs each touched spec by file (alone; 4353–4359
  free), then the full suite. A red is root-caused against the server logs
  before it is accepted as the recorded timing class.

  - **C-1 — the keystone reads (consumed by P4.D342):** `db::memories_read::

    `count_keystones(conn, character_id, about_character_id: Option<&str>,

- **C2 — the Storyteller wire W1 (P4.D343 builds, P4.D344 consumes;
  `crates/quilltap-core/src/api/types.rs` + `apps/web/src/app/core/core-
  contract.ts`):** `Request::ChatUpdate { …, turn_mode: Option<Option<Value>>
  }` (`double_option`, serde key `turnMode`, a sibling of `chat`; absent →
  untouched; `"rotation"` / `"storyteller"` → `set_turn_mode` (+ `abandon_
  storyteller_round(chat_id, "abandoned")` on leaving); `null` or anything
  else → `BadRequest "Validation error"`, nothing written) ↔ `ChatUpdate
  Request.turnMode?: 'rotation' | 'storyteller'`; the bag key `chat.
  storytellerInviteFilter: boolean` (non-boolean → the whole PUT refused).
  `Request::ChatStorytellerRound { chat_id }` → `Response::ChatStoryteller
  Round(Value)` = `{ "round": StorytellerRound | null }`. `Request::Chat
  StorytellerRun { chat_id, round_id: String, participant_ids: Vec<String>,
  order: Option<String>, keep_order: Option<bool> }` (keys `roundId`,
  `participantIds`, `order` default `"sequential"`, `keepOrder`; a non-enum
  `order` → `BadRequest "Invalid request"`) → an EMPTY reply (`Response::Ok`
  / `Null` — the web edge answers the SSE stream; `{ ended }` would be extra
  information v4 never answers); refusals `Conflict` 409 `That round is no
  longer open`, `NotFound` 404 `That seat is not on this round` / `Chat not
  found`, `BadRequest` 400 `Choose at least one bid, or let the round lie` /
  `Only a seat that bid to speak can be chosen` / `A Destroy bid must be
  chosen on its own`. `Request::ChatStorytellerAsk { chat_id, round_id,
  participant_id }` → `Response::ChatStorytellerAsk(Value)` = `{ "bid":
  StorytellerBid }` (the ANSWERED card). `Request::ChatStorytellerLie {
  chat_id, round_id }` → `{ "closed": true }`. Frames on `EventPayload::Chat
  (ChatEvent)`: `{"storytellerRound": StorytellerRound}`, `{"storytellerBid":
  {"roundId", "bid": StorytellerBid}}`, `{"roundOpen": true, "roundId"}`,
  `chainComplete` with `reason: "round_open"` (the literal; no enum change);
  a concurrent turn's frames carry `"participantId"` (when the frame lacked
  one) and `"concurrent": true` as the LAST two keys (`ChatEvent::Tagged`
  flattening inner then the two keys). The chat GET wire: `turnMode:
  'rotation' | 'storyteller'` (never null on the wire), `storytellerInvite
  Filter: boolean`, both after `isPaused`. Realtime topic `"storytellerRounds"`
  scoped by chat id → `chatKeys.storytellerRound(chatId) = ['chat', chatId,
  'storytellerRound']`. `LlmLogDto.storytellerRoundId?: string | null`; log
  type `"STORYTELLER_BID"`. `CustomToolLibraryEntry.effect: 'read' | 'write'`
  after `intervention` (T2). TS types `StorytellerBid` (key order
  `participantId, characterId, characterName, status, statedTier?, tier?,
  tierRaised, tools, plan?, reason?, talkativeness, silent, askedAt?,
  answeredAt?`), `StorytellerRound` (`id, chatId, triggerMessageId, status,
  bids, selection, closedReason, createdAt, updatedAt, closedAt`),
  `StorytellerSelection { participantIds, order: Order | null, chosenAt,
  names }`, `StorytellerBidRecord { roundId, tier, plan, toolsNamed }`,
  `StorytellerTier` (`simple | lookup | act | destroy`), `StorytellerOrder`
  (`sequential | simultaneous`), `TIER_LABELS` — field for field, optional /
  nullable preserved. P4.D344 codes to this with `it.skipIf(SERVER_LANDED)`
  gates.
- **C3 — the intervention wire `InterventionWire` (P4.D345 builds, P4.D346
  consumes; the BINDING copy is P4.D345's order):** dispatch verbs
  `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionsList","chatId"}` → `Response::
  CustomToolInterventions(Value)` = `{"holds": InterventionHoldView[]}`;
  `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionPreview","chatId","id","overrides"}` →
  `Response::CustomToolInterventionPreview(Value)` = `{"preview":
  InterventionPreview}`; `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionReask","chatId",
  "id","overrides"}` → `Response::CustomToolInterventionReask(Value)` =
  `{"preview", "consults": [{ok, output, prompt}]}`; `{"type":"chatCustom
  ToolInterventionResolve","chatId","id","decision"}` → `Response::CustomTool
  InterventionResolve(Value)` = `{"resolved": "deal" | "as-rolled" | "veto"}`.
  Errors: 404 `Held run not found`; 409 `That run has already been settled`;
  400 — `This tool consults no oracle`, every `checkOverrides` sentence
  (`parameter "${name}" is not open to intervention`; `the roll is not open
  to intervention`; `a dice roll is set by its faces, not a number`; `this
  roll is not dice`; `this roll takes ${diceCount} dice, not ${n}`; `the
  ${field} is not open to intervention`; `there is no outcome row ${i}`;
  `effect ${i} is not open to intervention`), `CustomToolRunError`'s message,
  the Zod issue sentences joined `; `, and at the HTTP edge `The request body
  is not JSON`. HTTP: `GET /api/v1/chats/{id}/custom-tools?action=
  interventions`; `POST …?action=intervention-preview | intervention-reask |
  intervention-resolve` with bodies `{id, overrides}` / `{id, overrides}` /
  `{id, decision}`; an unknown action → the existing `Unknown action: <x>`
  envelope. Shapes (key order as v4's `view.ts` / `interventions.ts`; absent
  optionals OMITTED): `InterventionHoldView {id, tool, title, characterName:
  string|null, entrance: "llm"|"trigger", triggerPhrase?, createdAt: ms,
  eligible: {fields: string[], params: string[], effects: number[]},
  hasCharacter, definition: {parameters, outcomes: [{when, state, message,
  brief?}], effects, revealOdds, hasLlm, diceSides?}, baseline: Intervention
  Preview, consults: [{ok, output, prompt}]}`; `InterventionPreview {run:
  InterventionRunView, staleAnswer, promptNow?, clampedParams: string[],
  warnings: string[]}`; `InterventionRunView {params, rollForm, notation?,
  raw, diceRolls?, diceBreakdown, rollSpan: RunSpan, value, valueSpan: RunSpan,
  outcomeIndex, tableOutcomeIndex?, state, message, chipLabel?, title,
  visibility, announcer: "pascal"|"character", llm?: {ok, output, prompt},
  effects: InterventionEffectView[]}`; `InterventionEffectView {index, kind:
  "write"|"wardrobe"|"inventory"|"log", label, fired, skipped?, value?,
  items?, log?}`; `RunSpan {min, max, minExclusive?: true, maxExclusive?:
  true}`; `InterventionOverrides {params?, roll?, dice?: number[], value?,
  llmPrompt?, llmAnswer?: {ok, output}, outcomeIndex?, message?, state?,
  chipLabel?, title?, visibility?, announcer?, effects?: Record<"<index>",
  EffectPin>}`; `EffectPin {mode: "auto"|"on"|"off", value?, items?:
  string[], log?}`; the decision `{kind: "deal", overrides} | {kind:
  "as-rolled"} | {kind: "veto"}`. Event: `RealtimeTopic::CustomToolInterventions`

- **C9 — the WCAG rules R1–R12 (P4.D358 writes; EVERY SPA lane applies to
  the files it owns and records a HANDOFF for the census):**
  - **R1 Modal dialog surface** (`dff76ebef`): the dialog CARD (never the
    overlay) carries `role="dialog"` — `role="alertdialog"` where the
    dialog-roles table says so — `aria-modal="true"`, `aria-labelledby=
    "<titleId>"` with `id="<titleId>"` on the visible title element (a
    per-instance id: `inject(UniqueId)` if the SPA has one, else a module
    counter `qt-dialog-<n>`); `tabindex="-1"`; the shared focus directive
    (`qtDialogFocus`, P4.D358's `ui/dialog-focus.ts`) for focus-in / Tab-trap
    / restore; Escape closes unless the host's busy gate says otherwise (keep
    v4's gate expression). For a `qt-modal` host: nothing — the primitive
    does it.
  - **R2 Non-modal floating surface** (`FloatingDialog`): `role="dialog"
    aria-modal="false" aria-labelledby`, `tabindex="-1"`, focus in on open
    and back to the opener on close, NO Tab trap, close button `aria-label=
    "Close"`, Escape inside closes it (topmost: `stopPropagation`), Escape
    elsewhere closes it only while `!document.querySelector('[aria-modal=
    "true"]')`, both skipping `defaultPrevented`.
  - **R3 Icon-only buttons** carry v4's `aria-label` byte: `Close`, `Cancel`,
    `Dismiss message`, `Close gallery`, `` Remove alias ${alias} ``, `Up one
    folder`, `Toggle model list` (+ `aria-expanded`).
  - **R4 Visible label ↔ control:** `<label for="<id>">` + `id="<id>"` on the
    control; never a bare `<span>` caption beside a control.
  - **R5 Heading-captioned control:** `aria-labelledby="<headingId>"` with
    `id` on the heading (ProfilesTab / ImageGenerationCard / ModelBehaviorCard
    / FieldSelectionStep / SuggestionCard / ContextCompression switch).
  - **R6 Placeholder-only / caption-less control:** `aria-label` with v4's
    string (never invent a string; where v4 has none, do not add one).
  - **R7 Switch:** `role="switch"` + `aria-checked` + a name (`aria-label` or
    `aria-labelledby`); the Prospero one is `type="button"` too.
  - **R8 Clickable non-button:** `role="button" tabindex="0" (keydown)=
    "activateOnKey($event)"` (P4.D358's `ui/activate-on-key.ts`) with
    `aria-expanded` (FileTable) / `aria-pressed` (gallery tile, quick-hide
    tags) where v4 sets them. Prefer a real `<button>` where the layout
    allows (v4 kept spans only inside `<tr>` / heading contexts and under
    the uppercase-button themes).
  - **R9 Live region for notices:** the toast container `role="status"
    aria-live="polite" data-toast-container`; an error toast `role="alert"`
    (P4.D358 only).
  - **R10 Colour-only state gets words:** `<span class="sr-only">Unmoderated
    chat.</span>`; ` (overheard)` appended to the whisper label.
  - **R11 Async result announced:** `<span class="sr-only" role="status"
    aria-live="polite">…</span>` (DocumentPane: `editingIndicator ??
    (saveLabel === 'Saved' ? 'Saved' : '')`).
  - **R12 Pointer targets ≥ 24 px** — CSS, P4.D357 (`.qt-tab-close`);
    templates: `min-w-6 min-h-6` on the inline copy-id button (P4.D358).
  The `e2e/support/toasts.ts` helper: `export const toastStack = (page:

  (defaulted `Ok(0)`; P4.D355 R-4 makes the WARN live); `chat_delete(db,
  probe, chat_id)`; `db/chats.rs::delete` runs `DELETE FROM terminal_sessions
  WHERE chatId = ?1` BEFORE the probe call; the ONE `engine.rs:3454` line
  (`ready_db()` → `ready_db_and_terminal_probe()`); the host's `terminate(id,
  grace_ms = 3000)` (SIGHUP → SIGKILL, the WARN) on both web routes,
  `kick_all_for_chat` DELETED. **C6 — the transcript listing (P4.D355):**
  `TranscriptStore::list_transcripts(&self) -> Result<Vec<TranscriptFile {
  session_id, path, mtime_ms }>, String>` (ENOENT → `Ok(vec![])` + the

  `orphanedThumbnailsSwept`, `ORPHAN_TERMINAL_TRANSCRIPT_GRACE_MS = 60 * 60 *
  1000` in `services/queue_service.rs`. **C7 — the mountinfo source
  (P4.D354):** `pub trait MountInfoSource { fn read_mount_info(&self) ->
  std::io::Result<String>; }` on `SyncDeps` (default `ProcMountInfo` reading
  `/proc/self/mountinfo`); `SyncRefusalCode::TargetNotPersistent`
  (`"TARGET_NOT_PERSISTENT"`, `is_conflict() == false` → 400 with the byte-
  exact message `The path '${targetPath}' is inside the container itself,
  not on a bind mount, so anything written there is lost when the container
  is recreated. Choose a path under the data directory (/app/quilltap) or
  under a store that is bind-mounted from the host (see 'quilltap docs
  docker-mounts').`); `is_docker` from `services/data_dir::is_docker_
  environment`; `browse_directory(path, default_dir)` with the ONE `engine.
  rs:4479-4481` line; `DocumentTextExtractor::is_available(&self) -> bool {

  (P4.D356):** `db/chat_settings.rs::default_settings_columns()` (`:1951`)

  field's WHOLE default from it (per top-level `path[0]`), `null` where the
  column's schema accepts null, else drops the key; `find_one_where` by user
  orders `updatedAt DESC` and scans for the first valid row; `update_for_
  user_returning_id` SETs the merged validated row.
